- [ ] Inbox dedup verifier found 2 duplicate envelope ids on acer (2026-04-28): added bounded read-only verifier `tools/behcs/inbox-dedup-check.mjs` (CLI: `[--archives] [--limit N]`, exit 0 clean / 1 duplicates / 2 runtime). Streams `data/behcs/inbox.ndjson` and optionally `data/behcs/inbox-archives/*`, counts envelopes per top-level `id`, prints `(ts, from, to, type, source)` tuples for any id with count > 1, and reports `total_lines | unique_ids | duplicates`. Live-inbox-only run: `total_lines=332, unique_ids=326, duplicates=2`. With `--archives` (18 files): `total_lines=8852, unique_ids=5759, duplicates=2` — same two ids, no NEW collisions in archives. Duplicates: (a) `id=e10ba926ad2cae4535473cfacaf894e4` count=2, both `liris -> acer` `FIRST_MESSAGE_CANON`, ts `2026-04-28T23:09:18.370Z` and `2026-04-28T23:16:13.310Z` (~7 min apart, real-history collision; replay or retransmit, not a key reuse); (b) `id=test-perTypeEnforce-malformed-001` count=2, both `from=test to=acer FIRST_MESSAGE_CANON` at identical ts `2026-04-29T01:03:00Z` (looks like a per-type-enforce malformed-input test fixture left in the live inbox). The bus is therefore NOT independently deduping by id at write time; it tolerates same-id writes. Side note: live inbox has `4` envelopes with no id field; archives contain `3091` lines without a top-level `id` field (older envelope shape — informational, not a dedup gap by itself). Follow-up but DO NOT fix in this cascade — deduplicating could delete real history (the 7-minute gap on the liris FIRST_MESSAGE_CANON pair may be a legitimate retransmit that the cosign chain depends on). Next-cascade options to consider before any deletion: (1) seal current inbox+archives to BEHCS so dedup is reversible, (2) decide policy — bus-side write-time dedup vs read-time dedup vs keep-all+canonical-pick — under operator cosign, (3) classify the `test-perTypeEnforce-malformed-001` pair as a fixture and quarantine to a test inbox rather than the live one, (4) decide whether the `4` no-id live envelopes (and `3091` no-id archive envelopes) need a synthetic id or a separate ledger. Boundary preserved: read-only verifier, no inbox mutation, no canonicalization, no envelope deletion, no archive mutation, no adapter/hook wiring.

- [x] Added installable mobile app support (PWA manifest + icons + install prompt in mobile console).

- [x] Added runtime `remoteBaseUrl` support for mobile routing and bootstrap URL generation (`/api/mobile/connections` + `/api/settings` + routing state).
- [x] Added clean-memory filtering for prompt/mobile history to suppress startup boilerplate noise (`/api/memory?clean=true`, prompt context cleanup).
- [x] Added mobile window-management controls (list/focus/minimize/maximize/restore/close) with new API endpoints.
- [x] Added multi-display support for phone captures (display list endpoint + selectable display/all-displays capture).
- [x] Diagnosed mobile "empty shell" concern: verified live brain job path is Codex-first and currently resolving to provider=`codex` for chat jobs.
- [x] Reduced repetitive "Asolaria Brain mode..." drift by tightening Codex prompt handling (including Windows-safe multiline flattening), expanding boilerplate-memory filtering, and blocking stale startup seed auto-runs from old trivial messages.
- [x] Improved mobile console chat transparency by showing job provider/provider-chain and source/provider tags in recent conversation entries.
- [x] Improved control connectivity with mobile diagnostics endpoint (`/api/mobile/control/diagnostics`) that returns display/window/routing/control health in one request.
- [x] Added resilient stale-window fallback for phone window focus/actions: if PID targeting fails, retry by window title automatically.
- [x] Added multi-display targeting for phone controls by forwarding `displayIndex` from mobile API through desktop connector to `Invoke-DesktopInput.ps1`.
- [x] Added GET-safe mobile API retry/backoff in mobile console client plus explicit network event history panel (offline/online/retry/recover/fail) for phone troubleshooting.
- [x] Added hardened local backend ops connector for `build/test/script` with allowlisted roots/aliases, no-shell command execution, and timeout/output caps.
- [x] Added backend headless browser task execution (`goto/click/type/press/wait/extract_text/extract_links`) with URL policy guards and result extraction.
- [x] Added new routes/endpoints for local/backend automation: `local build/test/script`, `browser task`, `GET /api/local/ops/status`, `POST /api/local/ops/run`, `POST /api/browser/task`.
- [x] Improved Python local build reliability by adding automatic `python` -> `py` fallback plus quiet/excluded `compileall` targeting (`backend/tests`) to avoid `.venv/.git` scans.
- [x] Added Slack backend integration (vault-backed token config + channel listing + conversation review/summarize) with no UI/browser dependency.
- [x] Added Slack safety controls to prevent risky cross-account behavior: URL redaction in transcript analysis, no-link-navigation instruction set, optional channel allowlist, and default DM disable policy.
- [x] Added work-link VPN guardrail for sensitive enterprise domains (Teams/Microsoft auth/sharepoint) so navigation is blocked unless required channel policy (default `vpn`) is active.
- [x] Added work-organization profile policy with runtime switching (`qdd`, `csi`, `emergencetek`) and org-aware Teams/VPN enforcement.
- [x] Added work-org APIs and routed commands: `GET /api/policy/work-orgs`, `POST /api/policy/work-org/select`, `work org status`, `work org set <org>`.
- [x] Hardened policy denial HTTP behavior so blocked work-link actions return explicit `403` instead of generic `500`.
- [x] Enforced strict VPN heartbeat requirement for Teams/Microsoft domains and added runtime heartbeat endpoint `POST /api/policy/channel/heartbeat`.
- [x] Added mobile console Chrome tab controls (`Prev Tab` / `Next Tab`) mapped to `ctrl+shift+tab` and `ctrl+tab`.
- [x] Fixed mobile display-selection persistence bug (`screenIndex`/`displayIndex` mismatch) that caused screen view to snap back to primary.
- [x] Added persistent `All Screens` toggle mode in mobile console (`All Screens Off` / `All Screens On`) instead of one-shot behavior.
- [x] Added phone Developer Options connector and APIs (`/api/phone/developer-options/*`, `/api/mobile/phone/developer-options/*`) with safe/risky catalog filters, per-option set, and queued sweep reporting.
- [x] Added mobile console Developer Options dashboard controls (catalog/status/sweep + per-option On/Off toggle buttons) wired to the new mobile endpoints.
- [x] Added Agent Colony completion watchdog digest logic to `getAgentColonyStatus` with done/result signal detection and 15-minute digest state reporting.

- 2026-02-24 12:34:59 -03:00: Investigated repeated startup autonomous run `close asolaria ui all`. Root cause: startup auto-run (`ASOLARIA_STARTUP_AUTORUN`, default true) calls `queueStartupAutomation()` on boot; seed is `ASOLARIA_STARTUP_PROMPT` or latest non-trivial user memory turn within `ASOLARIA_STARTUP_SEED_MAX_AGE_HOURS` (default 2h). Because `/api/chat` records routed commands as user turns, each execution of `close asolaria ui all` refreshed the seed and could retrigger on next startup. Routed path confirmed: `src/intentRouter.js` `asolaria_ui_close` -> `server.js` `closeTrackedUiWindows("all")`.
- 2026-02-24 12:34:51 -03:00: Investigated repeated startup `close asolaria ui all` runs. Root path in `server.js` (`resolveStartupSeedPrompt` lines 5956-5977 + `queueStartupAutomation` lines 6000-6037 + startup timer 17739-17747) auto-seeds from latest non-trivial user memory when `ASOLARIA_STARTUP_AUTORUN=true` (default) and age <= `ASOLARIA_STARTUP_SEED_MAX_AGE_HOURS` (default 2). Trigger observed from memory snapshots where startup brain then posts `close asolaria ui all` to `/api/chat`, creating new `user/chat` turns that become the next startup seed.
